.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:95:### 5.14 残余（凍結後の計測事実、D-024-29）
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:103:## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:105:orchestrator 側の仕組み（merge gate、integrate-merge、orch-gate、dispositions、audit-pool、verdict、chain）は、同じホスト上で同じ UID の worker プロセスと共存する。2026-10-19 の C-80 審査（a05、dispositions-a02、audit-pool-a01 の 3 文、計 19 件の P1）は、どの文も同じ前提を書かず同じ穴を持つことを示した。以後、`security: true` の orchestrator 側タスク文は本節を参照で適用し、本節の各項目に対応する threat 行を持つ。文ごとに言い換えない。
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:107:1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。 The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). The inventory of executable configuration sources a worker start screens is the documented loading list of the installed Claude Code version, enumerated once from the documentation (settings: user, project, local and managed `settings.json`; project `.mcp.json`; the configuration directory's global state `.claude.json` with user-scoped and project-local MCP servers; plugins and skills directories (`.claude/skills/*/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, enabled plugins); hook handlers of every documented type (`command`, `http` and any other `type` value the hooks reference lists: a handler is an executable input whether it names a command or a URL, and a worker start refuses every handler type but `command`), `apiKeyHelper`, `statusLine.command`, MCP server commands), and the launcher screens or disables every source before accepting workspace trust, refusing any executable payload it did not write; adding sources one review at a time is the anti-pattern (P1-15 a02 reviews 2ae9e2c and ebbb71b, 2026-10-19). The enumeration quotes the documentation's values (the page, its fetched_at and the listed values) in the text's premises so a reviewer can check them against the live page; naming a source by one of its fields ("hook commands" for hook handlers) is the same anti-pattern one level down (a02 review b2a186e, 2026-10-19T20:36Z; C-95).
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:108:2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:109:3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:114:8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly: `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` subcommands take `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL, and `gh api` (which has no `--repo` flag) takes the explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder; a `gh api graphql` call binds the repository through fixed query variables (`owner: "mryfmo"`, `name: "dotfiles"` as constants in the script, passed with `-f`/`-F`) and uses only node ids obtained from a query bound by those variables (verdict a04 C-80 round 5, 2026-10-19): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged. The sha of canonical `main` comes from the fixed repository's API (`gh api repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/git/ref/heads/main --jq .object.sha`), never from `git ls-remote origin`, whose destination the local remote and URL-rewrite configuration control (P1-12 a04 C-80, 2026-10-19T20:01Z); the content read at that sha comes from `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/contents/<path>?ref=<sha40>`. Every `gh api` call that decides also binds the host with `--hostname github.com` (`GH_HOST` and the hosts configuration select the API host otherwise), a subcommand binds it through the `github.com/mryfmo/dotfiles` form of `--repo`, and a test that exports a hostile `GH_HOST` shows the call still names github.com or refuses (a02 review b2a186e, 2026-10-19T20:36Z).
.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md:115:9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). A program takes `UV_CACHE_DIR` from the environment the trusted parent supplies and refuses when it is absent or not an absolute path; it never computes a default from its own root. The orchestrator's parent prefix sets the main checkout's `.agents/worklog/uv-cache`; a worker's launcher sets `<worktree>/.uv-cache` (the P1-15 worker launch value: `.agents/` is read-only in the Codex permission profile, so a root-derived `.agents/worklog/uv-cache` cannot be created by a worker; P1-12 a03, 2026-10-19T11:28Z); tests set a temporary directory.
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:75:C-80	text-only	code	pending	procedure:the security-profile review of a security: true task text and threat table runs before dispatch and its findings join the class table (M-20261019-03); tool:security-review.sh --text <task.md> (glue)
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:78:C-83	text-only	code	pending	procedure:a security: true orchestrator-side task text applies design 5.14 by reference with one threat row per item (D-024-18); tool:task-consistency-check.py C18 verifies the reference and the seven rows for kind=code tasks naming scripts/orchestrator or hooks (P1-12 owner)
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:80:C-85	text-only	code	pending	procedure:integration chains serialize on the one canonical review worktree today (chain-integrate.sh refuses any other path: "review worktree is not the canonical one", probed 2026-10-19T04:53Z with three extra worktrees); the security reviews, the C-80 text reviews and the redesigns run in parallel; the per-PR review worktree that lets chains run concurrently is a P1-6 item (after P1-15 under D-024-20); merges stay serialized through the gate (operator instruction 2026-10-19 13:5x: maximize parallel work without quality loss)
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:81:C-86	text-only	code	pending	procedure:a dispatched text that cites a design section or decision not yet on main names the absolute path of the orchestrator working copy where the worker reads it (worker sandboxes read the main checkout), or inlines the section; found 2026-10-19T05:45Z when P1-15 was dispatched citing design 5.14 that exists only locally; tool:task-consistency-check C20 verifies each cited section exists on origin/main or the text names the path (P1-12 owner); amended 2026-10-19T08:3xZ: the path is written in $HOME-relative form (`$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/…`), never as a literal `/Users/<user>/…`, because the validator's home-path scan (validate-agent-assets.py:1385-1428) refuses literal home paths in the committed task copy and masking the copy would break the task sha (P1-15 amendment 2, line 414; the a04 and a07 texts carried the same path)
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:83:C-88	text-only	code	pending	procedure:a dispatched text that relies on a design section not yet on main quotes the relied-on sentences verbatim inline, so the reviewed bytes (and the TASK/RESULT sha256) carry the contract; the path is context only; C-86's path-naming alone is insufficient (the a07 C-80 of 2026-10-19T07:30Z: changing the referenced design changes the effective instructions without changing the reviewed bytes); tool:task-consistency-check C20 requires a quotation block for every cited uncommitted section (P1-12 owner)
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:87:C-92	text-only	code	pending	procedure:a task text's pre-dispatch (C-80) review is capped at 3 rounds; at the third incorrect verdict the text is re-planned as a whole by another context that receives every finding so far (never a delta prompt) or the task is split, with a re-plan: line and a default decision in the map (plan 006 A1 '3 rounds then freeze'; M-20261019-05: 19 rounds on the P1-15 text); tool:text-security-review.sh refuses a fourth review of one task id without a re-plan: line newer than the third verdict (orchestrator glue, now)
.orchestration/learning/corrections-inventory.tsv:92:C-97	pending-tool	code	pending	procedure:ff-main.sh fetches origin/main over HTTPS with gh credentials (design 5.14 item 8), never `git fetch origin` (the SSH remote hangs inside the sandbox and fails outside it without a key; 2026-10-19T21:02Z the run hung 4 minutes before it was stopped); until then the orchestrator fetches `+refs/heads/main:refs/remotes/origin/main` over HTTPS first and runs a scratch copy without the fetch line; tool:the chain-review-scripts owner lineage (a07) carries the fix

.orchestration/acceptance/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md:25:- 2026-10-19T03:58:10Z: C-80 third review of the P1-15 text (`…-text-security-ecc7ab5.md`): `Verdict: incorrect`, five P1: (1) a sandboxed worker can swap an allowed directory for a symlink after the hook validated, so the unsandboxed file tool overwrites a trusted path (the race is an escape, not a self-harm); (2) the writable object database defeats sha pinning: a replaced intermediate tree object redirects `git show <sha>:path` to an attacker blob (tree traversal does not re-hash intermediate objects); (3) hook descendants still resolve `python3` and `env bash` through shims (herdr-agent-state.sh, delivery scripts); (4) the Herdr unix socket in the worker sandbox lets a sandboxed client request host execution (`pane run`) through the server; (5) a hook timeout or kill is fail-open unless the per-hook `onFailure: "block"` (Claude Code ≥ 2.1.295) is set.

**Avoiding runaway output**
codex
本文は、実行ファイルの選択と `GIT_DIR` 継承を後続タスクへ分けています。その切り分けに実在する担当タスクがあるか、今回「closed」とするテストの範囲と矛盾しないかを照合しています。読み取り専用の指定に従い、worklog やレビュー証跡ファイルは更新しません。

[Sandbox semantics](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/sandboxing#run-commands-outside-the-sandbox-with-excludedcommands).

- [P2] High confidence [.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:22](/Users/a0004262/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:22) — The environment-dictionary test cannot close executable selection: unchanged `CHECKER_RUN` resolves `uv` through this host’s mise-first PATH and does not pin Python, so credentials reach the selected launcher/interpreter before checker validation; `--no-project` still permits discovery of a local or parent virtual environment. [uv semantics](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/reference/cli/#uv-run--no-project).

- [P2] High confidence [.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:32](/Users/a0004262/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:32) — Retained “base-only execution” is not bound on this host: regime-ci’s Git reads inherit `GIT_DIR`, while execution opens the checker beneath `root`; a read-only probe returned this repository’s HEAD from `/tmp`, demonstrating that the HEAD decision need not identify the executed filesystem tree, even without a symlink race. [Git environment semantics](https://git-scm.com/docs/git#Documentation/git.txt-GITDIR).

- [P2] High confidence [.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:23](/Users/a0004262/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:23) — Workflow `contents: read` constrains its generated token, not arbitrary parent `GH_TOKEN`/`GITHUB_TOKEN` values preserved by line 67; a local write-capable credential retains that authority, so this residual does not establish the claimed absence of write or merge authority. [Token permissions](https://docs.github.com/en/actions/tutorials/authenticate-with-github_token#modifying-the-permissions-for-the-github_token).

- [P2] High confidence [.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:77](/Users/a0004262/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-regime-ci-token-a01.md:77) — The prescribed clean PATH omits uv, which existing fixtures launch by name; invoking the named workflow test under that environment reproduced `FileNotFoundError: 'uv'`, preventing the required green validation independently of implementation correctness.

📝 まとめ: Reviewed the text, every threat row, and related records without modifying files; full suites and live Linux sandbox behavior were not tested, and the execution-binding risks above remain unresolved.
Verdict: incorrectTwo findings in head `2cb584823ccf5f22069ea925b252d8cefb020844`:

- **P2 · High confidence — `scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py:476`:** C12 classifies only the resolved target, allowing an unnamed applied symlink such as `scripts/run → tests/fixtures/payload.txt` through a wildcard scope without a concrete threat row, although invoking the link can execute its target.
- **P2 · High confidence — `scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py:251`:** The missing-literal shortcut returns before examining directory symlinks, so `scripts/link/new.py`, with `link` pointing elsewhere inside the repository, bypasses the claimed rejection of internal directory aliases.

Both reproduced with the checked-in functions and in-memory filesystem models, without writes. The first returned no findings for a `.txt` target; changing its target to an applied `.py` file correctly produced C12. The second returned no findings for a missing file beneath an internal directory alias. These gaps undermine the threat table’s path-identity/classification controls and its **closed** “internal directory aliases” row. Classification should consider both the enumerated applied path and its confined target; prefix validation must precede the missing-file shortcut.

The remaining controls revealed no additional findings: C14 uses digest authorization and fixed operations without shell execution; YAML uses safe loading; canonical reads bind repository, host and validated commit; credentials travel through child environments. Unicode/collision and C16 controls were inspected. Parent execution, shared caches and same-UID interference remain disclosed residual risks.

Source/test hashes and the task-copy hash match their evidence records. No files were changed.

Not rerun: full suites, native filesystem fixtures or live CI; GitHub connectivity failed. The two demonstrated path-validation bypasses remain unresolved.

     2	
     3	`uv run --no-config --no-project --with pyyaml python -I scripts/orchestrator/regime-ci.py --base <ref> --head <sha>
     4	[--root <base checkout>]` runs from the base checkout and reads the head only through `git show`: it never checks out,
     5	imports or executes head content. Each task whose `.orchestration/<t>/` the diff `<base>...<head>` touches and that has a
     6	`task.md` copy at the head (of a reset_of lineage, only the latest copy) is checked: the base's task-consistency checker
     7	on the base's dispatched text, the copy against the dispatched `.orchestration/tasks/<t>.md` at the base, whose text is the policy
     8	(allowed_files, caps, reset_of), precheck.json's binding, allowed_files and the caps. Without dispatched text at the base
     9	the copy is unverifiable, a warning until REGIME_REQUIRE_DISPATCHED=1 makes it a violation; result.txt and precheck.json
    10	are the pull request's own claims. It prints one `regime-ci: <t>: <rule>: <detail>` line per violation and
    11	`regime-ci: warning: ...` lines, and exits 1 on any violation, 2 when git cannot list the diff, else 0 after
    12	`regime-ci: ok <n> task(s)`. A diff without a task copy passes as `no target (records-only)` only when every changed path
    13	is under `.orchestration/`; no branch name grants anything.

    33	# --no-config: a uv.toml or pyproject.toml the pull request adds cannot steer this run
    34	CHECKER_RUN = ["uv", "run", "--no-config", "--no-project", "--with", "pyyaml", "python", "-I", CHECKER]
    35	RUNS = (CHECKER, "scripts/orchestrator/regime-ci.py", ".github/workflows/regime.yml")

    38	class GitError(Exception):
    39	    pass

    42	def git(root, *args):
    43	    return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=root, capture_output=True, check=False)
    44	
    45	
    46	def listed(root, *args):
    47	    """Return the NUL-separated output of a git listing command, or raise GitError: no listing passes by being empty."""
    48	    out = git(root, *args, "-z")
    49	    if out.returncode:
    50	        raise GitError(f"git {' '.join(args)} failed: {out.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()[:200]}")
    51	    return [item for item in out.stdout.decode().split("\0") if item]
    52	
    53	
    54	def show(root, head, path):
    55	    """Return the bytes of path at head, or None when head has no such file."""
    56	    out = git(root, "show", f"{head}:{path}")
    57	    return out.stdout if out.returncode == 0 else None
    58	
    59	
    60	def sha256(data):
    61	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    62	
    63	
    64	def token(text, name):
    65	    m = re.search(rf"(?:^|\s){name}=(\S+)", text)
    66	    return m.group(1) if m else None
    67	
    68	
    69	def allowed(path, entry):
    70	    """Match an allowed_files entry: `<dir>/**` takes its prefix, `*` and `?` stay within one path segment."""
    71	    if entry.endswith("/**"):
    72	        return path.startswith(entry[:-2])
    73	    rx = "".join("[^/]*" if c == "*" else "[^/]" if c == "?" else re.escape(c) for c in entry)
    74	    return re.fullmatch(rx, path) is not None
    75	
    76	
    77	def caps(text, numstat):
    78	    """Return (violations, warnings) for the `## Check` sentence's caps against the numstat rows."""
    79	    check = text.split("\n## Check", 1)[1].split("\n## ", 1)[0] if "\n## Check" in text else ""
    80	    outside = re.search(r"at most (\d+) changed lines(?: \([^)]*\))? outside ([^(\n]*)", check)
    81	    tests = re.search(r"(\d+)\s+(?:added\s+|changed\s+)?(?:lines\s+)?under\s+`?tests/", check)
    82	    missing = [
    83	        f"no {n} cap in the copy's ## Check section" for n, m in (("outside", outside), ("tests/", tests)) if not m
    84	    ]
    85	    excluded = ["tests/", ".orchestration/"]
    86	    if outside:
    87	        excluded = re.findall(r"`([^`]+)`", re.split(r",\s*(?:and\s+)?\d", outside.group(2))[0]) or excluded
    88	
    89	    def counted(path, prefixes):
    90	        return any(path == p or (p.endswith("/") and path.startswith(p)) for p in prefixes)
    91	
    92	    out_lines = sum(a + d for a, d, p in numstat if not counted(p, excluded))
    93	    test_lines = sum(a + d for a, d, p in numstat if counted(p, ["tests/"]))
    94	    found = []
    95	    if outside and out_lines > int(outside.group(1)):
    96	        found.append(f"{out_lines} changed lines outside {', '.join(excluded)} exceed {outside.group(1)}")
    97	    if tests and test_lines > int(tests.group(1)):
    98	        found.append(f"{test_lines} changed lines under tests/ exceed {tests.group(1)}")
    99	    return found, missing

   102	def front(data):
   103	    """Return a copy's front matter mapping, or None when it is unreadable or not a mapping."""
   104	    try:
   105	        fm = yaml.safe_load(data.decode().split("\n---\n", 1)[0].lstrip("-\n"))
   106	    except (UnicodeDecodeError, yaml.YAMLError):
   107	        return None
   108	    return fm if isinstance(fm, dict) else None

   111	def precheck(root, head, t, result_text, exempt=()):
   112	    """Return the violations of precheck.json's binding, or None when the task carries no precheck.json."""
   113	    data = show(root, head, f".orchestration/{t}/precheck.json")
   114	    if data is None:
   115	        return None
   116	    try:
   117	        doc = json.loads(data)
   118	    except ValueError as error:
   119	        return [f"precheck.json is not JSON: {error}"]
   120	    found = []
   121	    bound = doc.get("head") if isinstance(doc, dict) else None
   122	    if not isinstance(bound, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", bound):
   123	        found.append(f"precheck.json head {bound!r} is not a 40-hex commit id")
   124	    elif bound != head:
   125	        # precheck.json cannot name the commit that adds it: it names the code commit, and the head may add only

   128	            found.append(f"precheck.json head {bound} is neither the head {head} nor its ancestor")

   130	            p

   132	            if not p.startswith((f".orchestration/{t}/", *exempt))
   133	        ]:

   135	                f"precheck.json head {bound[:12]} differs from the head outside .orchestration/{t}/: {', '.join(other)}"
   136	            )

   140	        )
   141	    return found

   143	
   144	def policy(root, base, head, t):
   145	    """Return (the policy bytes, its front matter or None, whether the base's dispatched text is the policy)."""
   146	    dispatched = show(root, base, f".orchestration/tasks/{t}.md")
   147	    data = show(root, head, f".orchestration/{t}/task.md") if dispatched is None else dispatched
   148	    return data, front(data), dispatched is not None

   151	def checker(root, data):
   152	    """Return the base checker's violations on the base's dispatched bytes: it hands the text's literals to git (C8 to
   153	    C10) and opens its design path (C6), so a pull request's own text never reaches it (worker review P0 of bcbd6949)."""
   154	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as scratch:
   155	        cmd = [*CHECKER_RUN, "--root", root, str(Path(scratch, "task.md"))]
   156	        Path(cmd[-1]).write_bytes(data)
   157	        run = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
   158	    out = run.stdout.splitlines()  # the file is task.md, so C1 compares task_id with `task`; task-id checks <t>
   159	    # only the checker's own verdict line with its exit code counts; a crash also exits 1, with no verdict line
   160	    if (out[:1], run.returncode) not in ((["task.md: OK"], 0), (["task.md: FAIL"], 1)):
   161	        return [("checker", f"no verdict (exit {run.returncode}): {(run.stdout + run.stderr).strip()[-300:]}")]
   162	    lines = [ln.strip() for ln in out[1:] if ln.strip()]
   163	    return [("checker", ln) for ln in lines if not re.fullmatch(r"C1 task_id .* != file task", ln)]
   164	
   165	
   166	def check_task(root, base, head, t, changed, numstat):
   167	    """Return (violations, warnings) for one task, each violation a (rule, detail) pair."""
   168	    copy_path = f".orchestration/{t}/task.md"
   169	    copy = show(root, head, copy_path)
   170	    if (fm := front(copy)) is None:
   171	        return [("front-matter", f"{copy_path} is unreadable or has no front matter mapping")], []
   172	    data, rules, dispatched = policy(root, base, head, t)
   173	    if rules is None:
   174	        return [("front-matter", f"the base's .orchestration/tasks/{t}.md has no front matter mapping")], []
   175	    found, warnings = [], []
   176	    if fm.get("task_id") != t:
   177	        found.append(("task-id", f"the copy's task_id {fm.get('task_id')!r} is not its directory {t}"))
   178	    result = show(root, head, f".orchestration/{t}/result.txt")
   179	    result_text = result.decode(errors="replace") if result is not None else ""
   180	    if dispatched:
   181	        if data != copy:
   182	            found.append(("task-copy", f"{copy_path} differs from the base's dispatched .orchestration/tasks/{t}.md"))
   183	    else:  # the copy's own policy is provisional: a head file or a result.txt token is a claim, never authentication
   184	        unverifiable = "task copy unverifiable (no dispatched text at base)"
   185	        found += [("task-copy", unverifiable)] if os.environ.get("REGIME_REQUIRE_DISPATCHED") == "1" else []
   186	        warnings += [] if os.environ.get("REGIME_REQUIRE_DISPATCHED") == "1" else [unverifiable]
   187	        warnings.append("checker skipped: the copy is unverifiable, and the base's checker reads only the base's text")
   188	        if result is not None and token(result_text, "task_copy") != sha256(copy):
   189	            found.append(
   190	                ("task-copy", f"sha256 {sha256(copy)} is not result.txt's task_copy={token(result_text, 'task_copy')}")
   191	            )
   192	    found += checker(root, data) if dispatched else []
   193	    exempt = tuple(f".orchestration/{p}/" for p in rules.get("reset_of") or [] if isinstance(p, str))
   194	    bound = precheck(root, head, t, result_text, exempt)
   195	    if bound is None:
   196	        warnings.append("precheck absent")
   197	    found += [("precheck", detail) for detail in bound or []]
   198	    entries = [a for a in rules.get("allowed_files") or [] if isinstance(a, str)]
   199	    found += [
   200	        ("allowed-files", f"{p} is outside allowed_files")
   201	        for p in changed
   202	        if not p.startswith(exempt) and not any(allowed(p, a) for a in entries)
   203	    ]
   204	    exceeded, cap_warnings = caps(data.decode(errors="replace"), numstat)
   205	    found += [("caps", detail) for detail in exceeded]
   206	    return found, warnings + cap_warnings
   207	
   208	
   209	def main(argv=None):
   210	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
   211	    parser.add_argument("--base", required=True, help="the base ref, such as origin/main")
   212	    parser.add_argument("--head", required=True, help="the pull request's head commit")
   213	    parser.add_argument("--root", help="the base checkout (default: the current git toplevel)")
   214	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   215	    root = args.root or git(".", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel").stdout.decode().strip() or "."
   216	    shas = [
   217	        git(root, "rev-parse", "--verify", "--quiet", f"{r}^{{commit}}").stdout.decode().strip()
   218	        for r in (args.base, args.head)
   219	    ]
   220	    if not all(shas):
   221	        print(f"regime-ci: cannot resolve --base {args.base} or --head {args.head} in {root}")
   222	        return 2
   223	    base, head = shas
   224	    at = git(root, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.decode().strip()  # the verifier and the checker must be the base's
   225	    if at != base:
   226	        print(
   227	            f"regime-ci: checkout: {root} is at {at[:12]}, not the base {base[:12]}, so the code run would not be the base's"
   228	        )
   229	        return 1
   230	    try:
   231	        changed = listed(root, "diff", "--name-only", "--no-renames", f"{base}...{head}")
   232	        numstat = []
   233	        for row in listed(root, "diff", "--numstat", "--no-renames", f"{base}...{head}"):
   234	            added, deleted, path = row.split("\t", 2)  # a path may hold a tab; a binary file counts 0 lines
   235	            numstat.append((int(added) if added.isdigit() else 0, int(deleted) if deleted.isdigit() else 0, path))
   236	    except GitError as error:
   237	        print(f"regime-ci: {error}")
   238	        return 2
   239	    for path in sorted(set(changed) & set(RUNS)):
   240	        print(
   241	            f"regime-ci: warning: the diff changes {path}; this run executed the base's version, the audit reads the change"
   242	        )
   243	    dirs = sorted({m.group(1) for p in changed if (m := re.match(r"\.orchestration/([^/]+)/", p))})
   244	    targets = [t for t in dirs if show(root, head, f".orchestration/{t}/task.md") is not None]
   245	    if not targets:
   246	        stray = [p for p in changed if not p.startswith(".orchestration/")]
   247	        if not stray:  # content alone decides: a branch name is the pull request's own claim
   248	            print("regime-ci: no target (records-only)")
   249	            return 0
   250	        print(
   251	            f"regime-ci: no target: {len(stray)} changed path(s) outside .orchestration/ and no task copy, first {stray[0]}"
   252	        )
   253	        return 1
   254	    # Amendment 1: a later copy whose reset_of names an earlier one is the lineage's only target, when both verify
   255	    policies = {t: policy(root, base, head, t) for t in targets}
   256	    exact = {t: policies[t][2] and show(root, head, f".orchestration/{t}/task.md") == policies[t][0] for t in targets}
   257	    later = {
   258	        p: t
   259	        for t, (_, rules, _) in policies.items()
   260	        for p in (rules or {}).get("reset_of") or []
   261	        if p in policies and p != t and exact[t] and exact[p]  # both head copies equal their base texts
   262	    }
   263	    for p, t in sorted(later.items()):
   264	        print(f"regime-ci: warning: {p}: predecessor copy .orchestration/{p}/task.md (reset_of of {t})")
   265	    targets = [t for t in targets if t not in later]
   266	    failed = False
   267	    for t in targets:
   268	        try:
   269	            found, warnings = check_task(root, base, head, t, changed, numstat)
   270	        except GitError as error:
   271	            found, warnings = [("git", str(error))], []
   272	        for detail in warnings:
   273	            print(f"regime-ci: warning: {t}: {detail}")
   274	        for rule, detail in found:
   275	            print(f"regime-ci: {t}: {rule}: {detail}")
   276	        failed |= bool(found)
   277	    if not failed:
   278	        print(f"regime-ci: ok {len(targets)} task(s)")
   279	    return int(failed)
   280	
   281	
   282	if __name__ == "__main__":
   283	    sys.exit(main())
name: regime

# The regime job (plan 024 P1-11, redesigned in T133 regime-ci a02): a pull request that carries a task copy is checked
# by the base's scripts/orchestrator/regime-ci.py against the base's dispatched policy. pull_request_target runs this
# workflow as it is on the base; the pull request's head is fetched as data and is never checked out or executed.
# It is an optional check until the operator adds `regime` to the ruleset's required contexts.
on:
  pull_request_target:
    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened, ready_for_review]

concurrency:
  group: regime-pr-${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  regime:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
          # The base branch's tip at run time: the event's recorded base sha can lag the branch (on PR #420 it was the
          # head's fork point, from before this verifier; ci:39185982423:1). The pull request cannot move this branch.
          ref: refs/heads/${{ github.base_ref }}
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@c18668ad3cf93ea998bef934396af7bb5c839dc7 # v10.2.0
        with:
          enable-cache: false
      - name: Fetch the pull request's head as data
        env:
          PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
          HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}
        run: |
          git fetch --no-tags origin "refs/pull/${PR_NUMBER}/head:refs/regime/pr-head"
          test "$(git rev-parse refs/regime/pr-head)" = "${HEAD_SHA}"
      - name: Check the pull request with the base's verifier
        env:
          HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}
        run: uv run --no-config --no-project --with pyyaml python -I scripts/orchestrator/regime-ci.py --base "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" --head "${HEAD_SHA}"

The new authenticated API dependency breaks the existing regime CI caller, and two coverage gaps allow incomplete threat tables to pass. Targeted read-only probes confirmed these issues; the full test suite was not run.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Supply authentication for regime CI's new API calls — scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py:182-182
  After this checker lands on main, `.github/workflows/regime.yml` invokes it through `regime-ci.py` without setting `GH_TOKEN` or `GITHUB_TOKEN`. Fresh runners have no GitHub CLI login, so tasks referencing a design or requiring C8/C9 lookups now fail: unauthenticated `gh api` exits 4, and regime-ci reports `no verdict`. Supply the workflow token alongside this change and cover that caller environment in a regression test.

- [P2] Require a separate row for overlapping directory scopes — scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py:348-350
  With both `scripts/` and `scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py` allowed, a table containing only the child file's row passes. This prefix check credits that row to the directory, while the new `hits()` logic correctly credits it only to the child; consequently, nothing requires a row controlling the rest of `scripts/`. Validate coverage against each entry's own row, preserving the one-row-per-entry requirement in [AGENTS.md:78](AGENTS.md#L78).

- [P2] Classify Claude instruction shims as applied files — scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py:476-480
  For an allowed `*.md` scope, adding a concrete row for `AGENTS.md` makes C12 pass even without a row for the existing `CLAUDE.md`. That file controls Claude's instruction import and recovery behavior, as established by [AGENTS.md:5–6](AGENTS.md#L5-L6), so it is not ordinary documentation. Scoped instruction files such as `vendor/compactiondb/AGENTS.md` are likewise missed. Include these instruction files in applied-file classification so wildcard scopes require concrete threat rows.
The new authenticated API dependency breaks the existing regime CI caller, and two coverage gaps allow incomplete threat tables to pass. Targeted read-only probes confirmed these issues; the full test suite was not run.

101: 結論: 第 1 段階で消えるのは今の直列点（S1 の一部、S2、S8 の一部）。残る天井は S3（hosted runner）、S4/S5（モデルと監査の枠）、S7（hot spot）で、これらは席数の上限 D10（4 → 6）の根拠になる。6 を超える並列は、S3 と S5 の実測が出るまで plan に入れない。
102: 
103: ## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）
104: 
105: orchestrator 側の仕組み（merge gate、integrate-merge、orch-gate、dispositions、audit-pool、verdict、chain）は、同じホスト上で同じ UID の worker プロセスと共存する。2026-10-19 の C-80 審査（a05、dispositions-a02、audit-pool-a01 の 3 文、計 19 件の P1）は、どの文も同じ前提を書かず同じ穴を持つことを示した。以後、`security: true` の orchestrator 側タスク文は本節を参照で適用し、本節の各項目に対応する threat 行を持つ。文ごとに言い換えない。
106: 
107: 1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。 The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). The inventory of executable configuration sources a worker start screens is the documented loading list of the installed Claude Code version, enumerated once from the documentation (settings: user, project, local and managed `settings.json`; project `.mcp.json`; the configuration directory's global state `.claude.json` with user-scoped and project-local MCP servers; plugins and skills directories (`.claude/skills/*/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, enabled plugins); hook handlers of every documented type (`command`, `http` and any other `type` value the hooks reference lists: a handler is an executable input whether it names a command or a URL, and a worker start refuses every handler type but `command`), `apiKeyHelper`, `statusLine.command`, MCP server commands), and the launcher screens or disables every source before accepting workspace trust, refusing any executable payload it did not write; adding sources one review at a time is the anti-pattern (P1-15 a02 reviews 2ae9e2c and ebbb71b, 2026-10-19). The enumeration quotes the documentation's values (the page, its fetched_at and the listed values) in the text's premises so a reviewer can check them against the live page; naming a source by one of its fields ("hook commands" for hook handlers) is the same anti-pattern one level down (a02 review b2a186e, 2026-10-19T20:36Z; C-95).
108: 2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
109: 3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
110: 4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
111: 5. **出力と予約の同一性** 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。
112: 6. **正直な残余** 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。
113: 0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
114: 8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly: `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` subcommands take `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL, and `gh api` (which has no `--repo` flag) takes the explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder; a `gh api graphql` call binds the repository through fixed query variables (`owner: "mryfmo"`, `name: "dotfiles"` as constants in the script, passed with `-f`/`-F`) and uses only node ids obtained from a query bound by those variables (verdict a04 C-80 round 5, 2026-10-19): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged. The sha of canonical `main` comes from the fixed repository's API (`gh api repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/git/ref/heads/main --jq .object.sha`), never from `git ls-remote origin`, whose destination the local remote and URL-rewrite configuration control (P1-12 a04 C-80, 2026-10-19T20:01Z); the content read at that sha comes from `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/contents/<path>?ref=<sha40>`. Every `gh api` call that decides also binds the host with `--hostname github.com` (`GH_HOST` and the hosts configuration select the API host otherwise), a subcommand binds it through the `github.com/mryfmo/dotfiles` form of `--repo`, and a test that exports a hostile `GH_HOST` shows the call still names github.com or refuses (a02 review b2a186e, 2026-10-19T20:36Z).
115: 9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). A program takes `UV_CACHE_DIR` from the environment the trusted parent supplies and refuses when it is absent or not an absolute path; it never computes a default from its own root. The orchestrator's parent prefix sets the main checkout's `.agents/worklog/uv-cache`; a worker's launcher sets `<worktree>/.uv-cache` (the P1-15 worker launch value: `.agents/` is read-only in the Codex permission profile, so a root-derived `.agents/worklog/uv-cache` cannot be created by a worker; P1-12 a03, 2026-10-19T11:28Z); tests set a temporary directory.
116: 10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
117: 7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

"""Exercise regime-ci.py on temporary repositories (a base with the dispatched text, a head with its copy) and regime.yml."""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
CHECKER = "scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py"
VERIFIER = "scripts/orchestrator/regime-ci.py"
UV = [
    shutil.which("uv") or "uv",
    "run",
    "--no-config",
    "--no-project",
    "--with",
    "pyyaml",
    "python",
    "-I",
]  # as regime.yml
T = "t1-a01"
RESIDUAL = '"residual: the fixture row is test data for this control"'
ROWS = "".join(
    f"  - control: {name}\n    disposition: {RESIDUAL}\n"
    for name in (
        "the PR edits src/tool.sh",
        "the PR edits tests/unit/test_tool.py",
        f"the evidence under .orchestration/{T}/",
        "task metadata",
        "metadata separators",
        "in-process effects",
        "workflow and interpreter or environment inputs",
        "new or renamed scripts",
        "orchestrator effect merge",
        "orchestrator effect update-branch",
        "orchestrator effect forced worker removal",
    )
)
TASK = (
    f"---\nformat: 2\ntask_id: {T}\nkind: code\nsecurity: false\nallowed_files:\n  - src/tool.sh\n"
    f"  - tests/unit/test_tool.py\n  - .orchestration/{T}/**\nthreat_table:\n{ROWS}premises: []\n---\n\n# {T}\n\n"
    "## Check\n\nat most 3 changed lines outside `tests/` and `.orchestration/`, 2 under `tests/`.\n"
)
CODE = {"src/tool.sh": "a\nb\nc\n", "tests/unit/test_tool.py": "x = 1\ny = 2\n"}
SKIPPED = f"regime-ci: warning: {T}: checker skipped: the copy is unverifiable, and the base's checker reads only the base's text"
GIT_ENV = {f"GIT_{role}_{k}": v for role in ("AUTHOR", "COMMITTER") for k, v in (("NAME", "t"), ("EMAIL", "t@t"))}


class RegimeCiTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k != "GITHUB_HEAD_REF"}
        self.env = env | GIT_ENV | {"GITHUB_HEAD_REF": "tools/t1"}

    def git(self, *args):
        cmd = ["git", "-c", "commit.gpgsign=false", *args]
        return subprocess.run(
            cmd, cwd=self.repo, env=self.env, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True
        ).stdout.strip()

    def commit(self, files, message):
        for rel, text in files.items():  # None deletes the file
            (self.repo / rel).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            (self.repo / rel).unlink() if text is None else (self.repo / rel).write_text(text)
        self.git("add", "-A")
        self.git("commit", "-q", "--allow-empty", "-m", message)
        return self.git("rev-parse", "HEAD")

    def build(
        self, copy=TASK, dispatched=TASK, code=None, precheck_head=None, precheck_token=None, evidence=True, **more
    ):
        """A base commit (the checker, the dispatched text), then a code commit and an evidence commit on a branch.

        more: `late` files committed after the code commit precheck.json names, `precheck_doc` as precheck.json's text,
        `task_copy` as result.txt's token, `checker` as the base's checker, `base` as more base files, `branch` as the
        head branch; precheck_head "side" names a commit on another branch. The base also holds the verifier, the
        checkout returns to the base, and the base's verifier runs against the head commit (a02).
        """
        temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temp.cleanup)
        self.repo = Path(temp.name).resolve()
        self.git("init", "-q", "-b", "main")
        base = {CHECKER: more.get("checker") or (ROOT / CHECKER).read_text(), "README": "base\n"} | more.get("base", {})
        base |= {VERIFIER: (ROOT / VERIFIER).read_text()}
        self.commit(base | ({f".orchestration/tasks/{T}.md": dispatched} if dispatched else {}), "base")
        self.git("update-ref", "refs/remotes/origin/main", "HEAD")  # the checker's C8 to C10 probe origin/main
        if precheck_head == "side":
            precheck_head = self.commit({"side.txt": "side\n"}, "side")
            self.git("reset", "-q", "--hard", "HEAD~1")
        self.git("switch", "-q", "-c", more.get("branch", "pr"))
        code_head = self.commit(CODE if code is None else code, "code")
        if "late" in more:
            self.commit(more["late"], "a change after the precheck")
        if evidence:
            pre = more.get("precheck_doc") or json.dumps({"head": precheck_head or code_head})
            token = precheck_token or hashlib.sha256(pre.encode()).hexdigest()
            copy_token = more.get("task_copy") or hashlib.sha256(copy.encode()).hexdigest()
            result = f"AGMSG-RESULT v1 task_id={T} precheck={token} task_copy={copy_token}\n"
            files = {f".orchestration/{T}/task.md": copy}
            if precheck_head != "absent":
                files |= {f".orchestration/{T}/precheck.json": pre, f".orchestration/{T}/result.txt": result}
            self.commit(files, "evidence")
        self.head = self.git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
        self.git("switch", "-q", "main")
        return self.regime()

    def head_commit(self, files, message):
        """Commit on the head branch and return the checkout to the base."""
        self.git("switch", "-q", "pr")
        self.head = self.commit(files, message)
        self.git("switch", "-q", "main")

    def regime(self, head=None, **env):
        cmd = [*UV, str(self.repo / VERIFIER), "--base", "main", "--head", head or self.head, "--root", str(self.repo)]
        return subprocess.run(
            cmd, cwd=self.repo, env=self.env | env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120, check=False
        )

    def test_regime_ci_reads_the_head_as_data_and_runs_the_base_verifier(self):
        # Bot P1 4308651010 and 4308651011 (a02 items 5, 6): the head rewrites the verifier and the checker into programs
        # that pass and leave markers, and forges a dispatched text with a matching result token; the base's policy,
        # verifier and checker still decide, the checkout stays at the base, and no head program runs.
        fake = 'import pathlib\npathlib.Path("head-program-ran").write_text("x")\nprint("{line}")\n'
        scripts = {VERIFIER: fake.format(line="regime-ci: ok 1 task(s)"), CHECKER: fake.format(line="task.md: OK")}
        rows = "".join(f"  - control: the PR edits {path}\n    disposition: {RESIDUAL}\n" for path in scripts)
        policy = TASK.replace("threat_table:\n", "threat_table:\n" + rows).replace("at most 3 ", "at most 999 ")
        policy = policy.replace("  - src/tool.sh\n", "  - src/tool.sh\n" + "".join(f"  - {path}\n" for path in scripts))
        forged = policy.replace("  - src/tool.sh\n", "  - src/tool.sh\n  - stray.sh\n")
        cases = (  # head changes, expected exit, the violation's phrase
            (CODE | scripts, 0, None),
            (CODE | scripts | {"stray.sh": "x\n"}, 1, "allowed-files: stray.sh is outside allowed_files"),
            (CODE | scripts | {"stray.sh": "x\n", f".orchestration/tasks/{T}.md": forged}, 1, "task-copy: "),
    def test_the_regime_workflow_runs_base_code_only(self):
        # Bot P1 4308651010 (a02 items 1, 5): pull_request_target runs the base's workflow and verifier; the head is a
        # fetched ref read as data, never checked out or executed.
        read = "import json, sys, yaml; print(json.dumps(yaml.safe_load(open(sys.argv[1])), default=str))"
        cmd = [*UV, "-c", read, str(ROOT / ".github/workflows/regime.yml")]
        doc = json.loads(subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout)
        types = ["opened", "synchronize", "reopened", "ready_for_review"]
        self.assertEqual({"pull_request_target": {"types": types}}, doc.get("on", doc.get("true")))
        self.assertEqual(({"contents": "read"}, ["regime"]), (doc["permissions"], list(doc["jobs"])))
        job = doc["jobs"]["regime"]
        self.assertEqual(("ubuntu-24.04", 10, None), (job["runs-on"], job["timeout-minutes"], job.get("permissions")))
        group = doc["concurrency"]
        self.assertEqual(
            ("regime-pr-${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}", True), (group["group"], group["cancel-in-progress"])
        )
        steps = job["steps"]
        pins = [
            "actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1",
            "astral-sh/setup-uv@c18668ad3cf93ea998bef934396af7bb5c839dc7",
        ]
        self.assertEqual(pins, [s["uses"] for s in steps if "uses" in s])
        base = "refs/heads/${{ github.base_ref }}"  # the tip at run time, never a stale base sha (ci:39185982423:1)
        self.assertEqual({"fetch-depth": 0, "ref": base, "persist-credentials": False}, steps[0]["with"])
        self.assertEqual({"enable-cache": False}, steps[1]["with"])
        runs = [s for s in steps if "run" in s]
        fetch, verify = runs
        self.assertIn('git fetch --no-tags origin "refs/pull/${PR_NUMBER}/head:refs/regime/pr-head"', fetch["run"])
        self.assertIn('test "$(git rev-parse refs/regime/pr-head)" = "${HEAD_SHA}"', fetch["run"])
        line = 'uv run --no-config --no-project --with pyyaml python -I scripts/orchestrator/regime-ci.py --base "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" --head "${HEAD_SHA}"'
        self.assertEqual(line, verify["run"])
        head = "${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}"
        self.assertEqual(({"HEAD_SHA": head}, head), (verify["env"], fetch["env"]["HEAD_SHA"]))
        for step in steps:  # no step checks out, switches to or runs anything from the head
            self.assertNotIn("${{", step.get("run", ""))
            self.assertNotRegex(step.get("run", ""), r"git (checkout|switch|worktree|reset)|pr-head:|\./")
            given = json.dumps(step.get("with", {})).replace("refs/heads/", "")  # the base branch's namespace only
            self.assertNotRegex(given, r"head|merge_commit_sha|event\.after|pull/")
            self.assertFalse(step.get("uses", "x").startswith("./"))

    """Return (the policy bytes, its front matter or None, whether the base's dispatched text is the policy)."""
    dispatched = show(root, base, f".orchestration/tasks/{t}.md")
    data = show(root, head, f".orchestration/{t}/task.md") if dispatched is None else dispatched
    return data, front(data), dispatched is not None


def checker(root, data):
    """Return the base checker's violations on the base's dispatched bytes: it hands the text's literals to git (C8 to
    C10) and opens its design path (C6), so a pull request's own text never reaches it (worker review P0 of bcbd6949)."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as scratch:
        cmd = [*CHECKER_RUN, "--root", root, str(Path(scratch, "task.md"))]
        Path(cmd[-1]).write_bytes(data)
        run = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
    out = run.stdout.splitlines()  # the file is task.md, so C1 compares task_id with `task`; task-id checks <t>
    # only the checker's own verdict line with its exit code counts; a crash also exits 1, with no verdict line
    if (out[:1], run.returncode) not in ((["task.md: OK"], 0), (["task.md: FAIL"], 1)):
        return [("checker", f"no verdict (exit {run.returncode}): {(run.stdout + run.stderr).strip()[-300:]}")]
    lines = [ln.strip() for ln in out[1:] if ln.strip()]
    return [("checker", ln) for ln in lines if not re.fullmatch(r"C1 task_id .* != file task", ln)]


def check_task(root, base, head, t, changed, numstat):
    """Return (violations, warnings) for one task, each violation a (rule, detail) pair."""
    copy_path = f".orchestration/{t}/task.md"
    copy = show(root, head, copy_path)
    if (fm := front(copy)) is None:
        return [("front-matter", f"{copy_path} is unreadable or has no front matter mapping")], []
    data, rules, dispatched = policy(root, base, head, t)
    if rules is None:
        return [("front-matter", f"the base's .orchestration/tasks/{t}.md has no front matter mapping")], []
    found, warnings = [], []
